Boundaries copied every iteration. With domain decomposition along z, interior z boundaries between ranks: those planes are interior points globally and need halo values from neighbors. Need each rank to store local z range [z0, z1) including halo layers? Let's design carefully.\n\nLet each rank own global z-slab of size nz_local = partition of nz. For stencil interior points z=1..nz-2, a rank computing local owned planes may need neighbor planes from adjacent ranks. Easiest: allocate local grid with 2 halo planes: local dimensions nx * ny * (nz_local + 2), with zlocal index 1..nz_local mapping to global z = z0 + (zlocal-1). Halo planes at 0 and nz_local+1. Owned planes correspond to global z0..z0+nz_local-1.\n\nAfter each iteration, exchange top/bottom owned planes with neighbors: send local plane z=1 (global lowest) to previous, and plane z=nz_local (global highest) to next; receive into halo z=0 and z=nz_local+1. Boundaries: global z=0 plane (owned by rank 0) is boundary and copied unchanged; global z=nz-1 plane (owned by last rank) boundary. For internal owner planes at global z=0 or nz-1, no stencil computation; copy. For x/y boundaries all ranks copy.\n\nOrder of operations: initialize global plane values based on global idx = z*(nx*ny)+y*nx+x. So local owned plane initialization needs global z. For halo, initialize too maybe by global z, but later exchanged. Since only interior points used, halos get filled by exchange. For safer initialize halos based on global z too. Halo at rank 0 z0-1 = -1 invalid; set 0. Fine.\n\nEach iteration:\n1. MPI halo exchange of current owned boundary planes.\n2. Compute output owned planes: for each owned global z:\n   - if global z ==0 or nz-1: copy input plane to output (boundary).\n   - else for y in 1..ny-2, x in 1..nx-2: stencil.\n   - boundaries x/y copy.\n   Wait global z interior but x/y boundaries handled per plane.\n3. planeBytes = plane * sizeof(Real);\n    const int planeCount = static_cast<int>(plane);\n\n    if (prevRank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(sendLower, deviceGrid + plane, planeBytes,\n                              cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n    }\n    if (nextRank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(sendUpper, deviceGrid + nzLocal * plane, planeBytes,\n                              cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n    }\n\n    // Send my lower plane to prev; receive my upper halo from next.\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Sendrecv(sendLower, planeCount, MPI_DOUBLE, prevRank, 11,\n                           recvUpper, planeCount, MPI_DOUBLE, nextRank, 11,\n                           comm, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE));\n\n    // Send my upper plane to next; receive my lower halo from prev.\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Sendrecv(sendUpper, planeCount, MPI_DOUBLE, nextRank, 12,\n                           recvLower, planeCount, MPI_DOUBLE, prevRank, 12,\n                           comm, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE));\n\n    if (prevRank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceGrid, recvLower, planeBytes,\n                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n Values should be reasonable (bounded)\n    Real minVal = grid[0];\n    Real maxVal = grid[0];\n    #pragma omp parallel for reduction(min:minVal) reduction(max:maxVal) schedule(static)\n    for (long long i = 0; i < count; ++i) {\n        const Real val = grid[static_cast<size_t>(i)];\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded\n    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y Each block\n// stages a small haloed x/y/z tile in shared memory so that every input value\n// is fetched from global memory only once per block instead of up to seven\n// times.\n__global__ void stencilKernel(const Real* __restrict__ input,\n                              Real* __restrict__ output,\n                              const int nx, const int ny,\n                              const int nzGlobal,\n                              const int z0,\n                              const int localZStart,\n                              const int localZCount) {\n    const int groupStart = localZStart + static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * kBlockZ;\n    const int remaining = localZStart + localZCount - groupStart;\n    if (remaining <= 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n    const int computed = remaining < kBlockZ ? remaining : kBlockZ;\n\n    constexpr int sharedX = kBlockX + 2;\n    constexpr int sharedY = kBlockY + 2;\n    constexpr int sharedPlane = sharedX * sharedY;\n    __shared__ Real tile[(kBlockZ + 2) * sharedPlane];\n\n    const int tid = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x);\n    const int numThreads = static_cast<int>(blockDim.x * blockDim.y);\n    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(nx) * static_cast<size_t>(ny);\n\n    const int xBase = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * kBlockX;\n    const int yBase = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * kBlockY;\n    const int tileZCount = computed + 2;\n    const int tileElems = tileZCount * sharedPlane;\n\n    for (int e = tid;
